问题与改进

每条按等级与类型标注，展开可看触发条件、实际观察、验收标准、证据原文与可直接粘贴的上游 Issue 草稿。评审发现不等于本轮实际阻塞，也不因业务通过而标为已解决。

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 37859683993 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 3

重要能力缺口上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · defineAIEmployee 无法声明 enabledTools，注册期员工工具边界缺失

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/ai-employee · @nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

候选成立（capability-gap）。AIEmployeeOptions/defineAIEmployee 只暴露 skills/tools，没有 enabledTools/enabledSkills；registerEmployee 经 toBuiltInEmployee 只写 skills/tools，enabledTools 仅能从已存在行或内部 sourceSettings 继承。运行时的真正外边界是 context.js 的 isToolSelected 读取 skillSettings.enabledTools，未保存选择时放行全部 GENERAL 工具。App 因此只能注册后 getAIEmployee + upsertEmployee 回写 enabledTools，属绕行而非误用。框架确缺注册期声明工具白名单的一等公民能力。

  • 职责与公开约定：冻结契约只为 defineAIEmployee/AIEmployeeOptions 暴露 skills 与 tools（元素仅 name/autoCall），没有 enabledTools/enabledSkills；这些字段只存在于 AIEmployeeSkillSettings，公开 registerEmployee 不会写入。 E7 E8
  • 实际行为证据：registerEmployeeInRepository 经 toBuiltInEmployee 只写 skills/tools；运行时代码以 isToolSelected 读 skillSettings.enabledTools 作外边界，未保存选择时 legacy 过滤放行全部 GENERAL 工具。 E8 E9 E10
  • 应用接入与业务边界：App 未误用 API：它先 registerEmployee，再因 API 无法声明而 getAIEmployee + upsertEmployee 回写 enabledTools=['search-materials']，说明缺的是框架注册期声明能力。 E12
  • 环境因素：差距由冻结的类型定义/源码直接决定，无需外部服务、凭据或依赖状态；未保存选择时放行 GENERAL 工具是代码默认路径，非环境副作用。 E7 E10
  • 工厂因素：工厂随附的 ai-employee 包与 Skill 只提供 skills/tools 注册字段，App 只能在注册后绕行写库；构建/QA 步骤并非成因。 E7 E12
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：已有 upsertEmployee 能写 enabledTools，是注册后写库的公开 workaround，但无法在 defineAIEmployee 声明，故注册期白名单能力确实缺失。 E7 E8

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

AIEmployeeOptions（defineAIEmployee 的输入）只有 skills/tools，没有 enabledTools/enabledSkills；toBuiltInEmployee 生成的 skillSettings 也只含 skills/tools，而 enabledSkills/enabledTools 只在 registerEmployeeInRepository 从已存在行或内部 sourceSettings 参数继承，公开 registerEmployee 不会传该参数。运行时的真正工具外边界是 skillSettings.enabledTools（context.js:277-281）。因此 App 无法在注册声明中表达最小权限，只能 registerEmployee 后 getEmployee 再 upsertEmployee 写入 allow-list。

实际影响
App 声明的「只读只用一个工具」在注册期并不成立；未保存选择时所有 GENERAL 内置工具（表单填充、图表、数据查询、网页搜索、子代理、MCP）默认对该员工可用，增加误调用与越权风险，并要求业务侧额外写补偿代码。
建议改法
在 AIEmployeeOptions 增加 enabledTools/enabledSkills，并在 registerEmployeeInRepository 首次创建时写入；或在 defineAIEmployee 文档与类型中给出等价的一等公民写法（例如允许 tools 携带 enabled 语义）。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
App 用 defineAIEmployee 声明一个只含 search-materials 的只读员工。
应有能力 / 行为
注册声明即可把该员工的可调用工具限定为声明列表。
实际观察
enabledTools 未写入；未保存选择时 isToolSelected 放行全部 GENERAL 工具，需额外 upsertEmployee 收紧。
应用绕行与代价
registerEmployee 后 getEmployee，再 upsertEmployee({...employee, skillSettings:{..., enabledTools:['search-materials']}})，并自行判断是否已被管理员设置。
改进后的验收标准
defineAIEmployee({tools, enabledTools}) 后 GET /api/aiEmployees/{username} 的 skillSettings.enabledTools 等于声明列表，且该员工运行时不再出现未声明的 GENERAL 工具。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 6 条
packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/types.d.ts:18–43E7

AIEmployeeOptions (the defineAIEmployee input) declares username/category/description/skills/tools/chatSettings/profile/systemPrompt/sort, but no enabledTools or enabledSkills. AIEmployeeManager exposes registerEmployee and upsertEmployee.

export type AIEmployeeOptions = {
    username: string;
    category?: string;
    description?: string;
    skills?: string[];
    tools?: AIEmployeeToolSetting[];
    chatSettings?: AIEmployeeEntity['chatSettings'];
    avatar?: string;
    nickname?: string;
    position?: string;
    bio?: string;
    greeting?: string;
    systemPrompt?: string | null;
    sort?: number;
};
export type AIEmployeeFilter = {
    builtIn?: boolean;
    username?: string;
};
export interface AIEmployeeManager {
    getEmployee(username: string): Promise<AIEmployeeEntity | undefined>;
    listEmployees(filter?: AIEmployeeFilter): Promise<AIEmployeeEntity[]>;
    registerEmployee(options: AIEmployeeOptions): Promise<void>;
    switchRepository(repository: AIEmployeeRepository): Promise<void>;
    upsertEmployee(entry: AIEmployeeEntity): Promise<AIEmployeeEntity>;
    deleteEmployee(username: string): Promise<void>;
packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/default.js:80–104E8

registerEmployeeInRepository builds the stored record from toBuiltInEmployee; enabledSkills/enabledTools are only carried over from an existing row or a sourceSettings argument that the public registerEmployee never passes.

    async registerEmployeeInRepository(repository, options, sourceSettings) {
        const current = (await repository.findOne({
            filter: { username: options.username },
        })) ?? undefined;
        const value = this.toBuiltInEmployee(options, current);
        // Registration refreshes legacy arrays, never an administrator's override.
        for (const key of ['enabledSkills', 'enabledTools']) {
            const selection = current?.skillSettings?.[key] !== undefined
                ? current.skillSettings[key]
                : sourceSettings?.[key];
            if (selection !== undefined) {
                value.skillSettings[key] =
                    selection === null ? null : [...new Set(selection)];
            }
        }
        if (Array.isArray(value.skillSettings.enabledTools)) {
            // The manager cannot classify dynamic tools by scope. Keep all saved
            // settings; runtime only consults these permissions for CUSTOM tools.
            // Saved ASK must win over a subsequently registered ALLOW default.
            value.skillSettings.tools = [
                ...new Map([
                    ...value.skillSettings.tools,
                    ...(sourceSettings?.tools ?? []),
                    ...(current?.skillSettings?.tools ?? []),
                ].map((tool) => [tool.name, tool])).values(),
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:224–260E9

getAIEmployeeTools starts from every GENERAL tool plus getSkill/web search, then merges skillSettings.tools and skillSettings.enabledTools into the candidate set.

    async getAIEmployeeTools(discoveredToolMap) {
        if (this.chatSettings.enableTools === false)
            return [];
        const currentFrontendTools = await listCurrentFrontendTools(this.conversations, { sessionId: this.sessionId, frontendTools: this.frontendTools });
        const tools = await this.listTools({ scope: 'GENERAL' });
        const getSkill = await this.toolsManager.getTools(SYSTEM_TOOLS.GET_SKILL, {
            ctx: this.agentContext,
        });
        if (getSkill)
            tools.push(getSkill);
        if (this.webSearch === true) {
            const webSearch = await this.toolsManager.getTools(SYSTEM_TOOLS.WEB_SEARCH, {
                ctx: this.agentContext,
            });
            if (webSearch)
                tools.push(webSearch);
        }
        const generalNames = new Set(tools.map((tool) => tool.definition.name));
        const toolMap = discoveredToolMap ?? (await this.listToolMap());
        const configured = [
            ...(this.employee.skillSettings?.tools ?? []),
            ...(this.employee.skillSettings?.enabledTools ?? []).map((name) => ({
                name,
            })),
        ];
        if (await this.getKnowledgeBaseRetrieveTool())
            configured.push({ name: SYSTEM_TOOLS.KNOWLEDGE_BASE });
        for (const setting of configured) {
            if (!generalNames.has(setting.name)) {
                const tool = toolMap.get(setting.name);
                if (tool)
                    tools.push(tool);
            }
        }
        const eligible = await this.getEligibleToolNames(tools.map((tool) => tool.definition.name));
        return prepareToolsForFrontendConversation(tools.filter((tool) => eligible.has(tool.definition.name)), currentFrontendTools);
    }
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:277–293E10

isToolSelected makes skillSettings.enabledTools the outer boundary "including system tools"; when no selection is saved the legacy filter admits all GENERAL tools.

    isToolSelected(name) {
        const enabled = this.employee.skillSettings?.enabledTools;
        // Employee selection is the outer boundary, including system tools.
        if (Array.isArray(enabled) && !enabled.includes(name))
            return false;
        const settings = this.skillSettings;
        const filter = settings?.tools;
        if (!Array.isArray(filter))
            return true;
        if (!settings?.toolsVersion && filter.length === 0)
            return true;
        // Preserve the legacy session exception; it never bypasses employee policy.
        return ([
            ...listSystemTools(),
            LOAD_FRONTEND_TOOL_NAME,
            EXECUTE_FRONTEND_TOOL_NAME,
        ].includes(name) || filter.includes(name));
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/references/capabilities.md:467–467E3

capabilities.md: a tool that registers as GENERAL "reaches every employee whose tool selection has never been saved"; narrowing it for one employee means that employee's tool selection in AI settings or a session skillSettings allowlist.

A server's tools register as `GENERAL` tools named `mcp-<server>-<tool>`, so every employee whose tool selection has never been saved has them from the moment the server connects. Once an employee's tool switches are saved in AI settings, that selection is a fixed list, and a tool discovered afterwards — a new MCP tool included — stays off for that employee until someone switches it on. Use that exact name wherever a tool is named: in a Skill's `tools`, an employee's `tools`, or a session's `skillSettings`. Narrowing them for one employee is its tool selection in AI settings, or a session's `skillSettings` allowlist.
app/server/ai/index.ts:28–52E12

App comment records that defineAIEmployee cannot express enabledTools, so restrictToolsToSearch reads the employee and upserts skillSettings.enabledTools = ['search-materials'].

/**
 * Pins the assistant's tool allow-list to its one read-only tool.
 *
 * `defineAIEmployee` cannot express `enabledTools`, and the AI plugin marks
 * every built-in tool it ships — form filling, charting, data queries, web
 * search and the sub-agent orchestration tools — `GENERAL`, which makes all of
 * them eligible for any employee that does not narrow the list. The materials
 * assistant must only ever be able to search materials, so its first
 * registration records the allow-list; a later administrator selection is left
 * untouched.
 */
async function restrictToolsToSearch(
  aiEmployeeManager: AIEmployeeManager,
  username: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const employee = await aiEmployeeManager.getEmployee(username);
  if (!employee || employee.skillSettings?.enabledTools != null) return;
  await aiEmployeeManager.upsertEmployee({
    ...employee,
    skillSettings: {
      ...employee.skillSettings,
      enabledTools: ['search-materials'],
    },
  });
}
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [重要][能力缺口] defineAIEmployee 无法声明 enabledTools，注册期员工工具边界缺失

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/ai-employee`（评审时 0.2.0-beta.8）、`@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.0）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

候选成立（capability-gap）。AIEmployeeOptions/defineAIEmployee 只暴露 skills/tools，没有 enabledTools/enabledSkills；registerEmployee 经 toBuiltInEmployee 只写 skills/tools，enabledTools 仅能从已存在行或内部 sourceSettings 继承。运行时的真正外边界是 context.js 的 isToolSelected 读取 skillSettings.enabledTools，未保存选择时放行全部 GENERAL 工具。App 因此只能注册后 getAIEmployee + upsertEmployee 回写 enabledTools，属绕行而非误用。框架确缺注册期声明工具白名单的一等公民能力。

- 职责与公开约定：冻结契约只为 defineAIEmployee/AIEmployeeOptions 暴露 skills 与 tools（元素仅 name/autoCall），没有 enabledTools/enabledSkills；这些字段只存在于 AIEmployeeSkillSettings，公开 registerEmployee 不会写入。；证据：E7（packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/types.d.ts:18–43）、E8（packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/default.js:80–104）
- 实际行为证据：registerEmployeeInRepository 经 toBuiltInEmployee 只写 skills/tools；运行时代码以 isToolSelected 读 skillSettings.enabledTools 作外边界，未保存选择时 legacy 过滤放行全部 GENERAL 工具。；证据：E8（packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/default.js:80–104）、E9（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:224–260）、E10（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:277–293）
- 应用接入与业务边界：App 未误用 API：它先 registerEmployee，再因 API 无法声明而 getAIEmployee + upsertEmployee 回写 enabledTools=['search-materials']，说明缺的是框架注册期声明能力。；证据：E12（app/server/ai/index.ts:28–52）
- 环境因素：差距由冻结的类型定义/源码直接决定，无需外部服务、凭据或依赖状态；未保存选择时放行 GENERAL 工具是代码默认路径，非环境副作用。；证据：E7（packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/types.d.ts:18–43）、E10（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:277–293）
- 工厂因素：工厂随附的 ai-employee 包与 Skill 只提供 skills/tools 注册字段，App 只能在注册后绕行写库；构建/QA 步骤并非成因。；证据：E7（packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/types.d.ts:18–43）、E12（app/server/ai/index.ts:28–52）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：已有 upsertEmployee 能写 enabledTools，是注册后写库的公开 workaround，但无法在 defineAIEmployee 声明，故注册期白名单能力确实缺失。；证据：E7（packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/types.d.ts:18–43）、E8（packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/default.js:80–104）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

AIEmployeeOptions（defineAIEmployee 的输入）只有 skills/tools，没有 enabledTools/enabledSkills；toBuiltInEmployee 生成的 skillSettings 也只含 skills/tools，而 enabledSkills/enabledTools 只在 registerEmployeeInRepository 从已存在行或内部 sourceSettings 参数继承，公开 registerEmployee 不会传该参数。运行时的真正工具外边界是 skillSettings.enabledTools（context.js:277-281）。因此 App 无法在注册声明中表达最小权限，只能 registerEmployee 后 getEmployee 再 upsertEmployee 写入 allow-list。

**影响**：App 声明的「只读只用一个工具」在注册期并不成立；未保存选择时所有 GENERAL 内置工具（表单填充、图表、数据查询、网页搜索、子代理、MCP）默认对该员工可用，增加误调用与越权风险，并要求业务侧额外写补偿代码。

**建议改动**：在 AIEmployeeOptions 增加 enabledTools/enabledSkills，并在 registerEmployeeInRepository 首次创建时写入；或在 defineAIEmployee 文档与类型中给出等价的一等公民写法（例如允许 tools 携带 enabled 语义）。

**验收标准**：defineAIEmployee({tools, enabledTools}) 后 GET /api/aiEmployees/{username} 的 skillSettings.enabledTools 等于声明列表，且该员工运行时不再出现未声明的 GENERAL 工具。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：

- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:224–260` — getAIEmployeeTools starts from every GENERAL tool plus getSkill/web search, then merges skillSettings.tools and skillSettings.enabledTools into the candidate set.
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:277–293` — isToolSelected makes skillSettings.enabledTools the outer boundary "including system tools"; when no selection is saved the legacy filter admits all GENERAL tools.
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/references/capabilities.md:467–467` — capabilities.md: a tool that registers as GENERAL "reaches every employee whose tool selection has never been saved"; narrowing it for one employee means that employee's tool selection in AI settings or a session skillSettings allowlist.
- `app/server/ai/index.ts:28–52` — App comment records that defineAIEmployee cannot express enabledTools, so restrictToolsToSearch reads the employee and upserts skillSettings.enabledTools = ['search-materials'].

F2 · Employee Skill 的「声明即工具列表」完成检查与实际运行时边界不一致

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

候选成立（guidance-gap）。SKILL.md 完成检查断言『员工工具列表正好显示所声明的』，api-reference 只把 enabledTools 注释为 roster 报告内容，均未说明 enabledTools 才是运行时工具外边界。实际 context.js 中未保存选择即放行全部 GENERAL 工具，收窄依赖 enabledTools。capabilities.md 已描述该机制，说明文档知情，但完成检查/API 参考未与之对齐，指引确有改进空间。

  • 职责与公开约定：SKILL.md 完成检查把『员工工具列表正好显示所声明的』列为验收项；api-reference 仅将 enabledTools 描述为『设置时 roster 报告的精确工具』，未声明它是运行时工具外边界。 E1 E5
  • 实际行为证据：运行时以 enabledTools 决定候选人选：未保存工具选择时 isToolSelected 放行全部 GENERAL 工具，声明 tools 只进入 legacy skillSettings.tools，故『显示=可调用』不成立。 E10 E8
  • 应用接入与业务边界：App 依据现有指引注册后仍须额外 upsert enabledTools 才收窄工具，印证指引未把 enabledTools 的外边界语义讲清。 E12
  • 环境因素：指引为冻结文本，结论不依赖外部环境；无需 QA/服务状态即可判断该完成检查的表述与运行时边界不对齐。 E1 E5
  • 工厂因素：不一致位于工厂分发的 Skill 指引本身（完成检查与 API 参考），属指引责任，而非构建脚本或提示注入故障。 E1 E5
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：capabilities.md 已说明 GENERAL 工具对未保存选择的员工可用、收窄需 AI 设置或 session skillSettings，说明机制已被文档掌握；但完成检查与 API 参考未与之对齐，改进空间成立。 E3

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

SKILL.md 的完成检查把「员工工具列表正好显示所声明的」作为验收项；api-reference 只在可编辑字段里把 enabledTools 注释为「roster 报告的精确工具」。但运行时外边界是 enabledTools，且声明 tools 只写入 legacy skillSettings.tools，当管理员未保存工具选择时 isToolSelected 会放行全部 GENERAL 工具（capabilities.md 第 467 行、runtime-extensions.md 第 65 行亦如此描述）。

评审时 · 文档 / API 声称

App 员工声明后，其工具列表「正好显示所声明的」，即可作为边界已生效的验收依据。

评审时 · 实际观察

注册声明不写 enabledTools；未保存选择时运行时放行全部 GENERAL 工具，enabledTools 才是有约束力的外边界。

实际影响
开发者按完成检查会误以为「声明列表 = 可调用工具集」，从而漏掉实际的最小权限收紧与负例验证；这正是本任务 App 需要额外写 restrictToolsToSearch 的原因。
建议改法
在 SKILL.md 与 api-reference 中明确 enabledTools 是运行时外边界（含系统工具）、tools 只是 legacy 列表；补充注册期设置边界的一等写法，并把完成检查改为校验实际可调用集合而非 roster 显示。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
开发者依据 Employee Skill 的完成检查判断声明即完成工具收敛。
应有能力 / 行为
声明的工具列表等于员工实际可调用的工具集合。
实际观察
未声明 enabledTools 时，运行时仍可调用全部 GENERAL 内置工具。
应用绕行与代价
阅读插件源码，使用 upsertEmployee 写 skillSettings.enabledTools。
改进后的验收标准
Skill/API 文档明确 enabledTools 的边界语义并给出注册期写法；完成检查校验实际可调用集合。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 5 条
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/SKILL.md:90–98E1

Completion checks for an App-defined AI employee. Line 94 asserts that after start "the employee's tool list shows exactly what was declared".

## Completion checks

- A first-time visitor lands on the page, sees a usable composer without switching employee or model, sends, and gets a reply. Reload and repeat: this tests a fresh mount, not a warm one.
- Each unhappy configuration shows its own actionable message rather than a composer that looks ready: discovery loading, discovery failed, no accessible employees, model discovery failed, no enabled models.
- The App employee, tool, and skill appear in AI settings after start, and the employee's tool list shows exactly what was declared.
- The new tool runs from chat, is approved when its permission is `ASK`, returns a serializable result, and leaves the expected database row behind. Run it twice and verify no duplicate.
- An unauthorized user is refused by the tool, not only by the prompt.
- The employee the page opens on is the one intended, not whichever sorts first.
- If the chat opens from a floating trigger: clicking the trigger opens the dialog or side panel, and closing it brings the trigger back. A surface that stays shut is holding its own `open` state instead of the controller's; see [chat-surfaces.md § Surfaces](references/chat-surfaces.md#surfaces).
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/references/api-reference.md:673–678E5

api-reference.md lists the editable employee skillSettings fields; enabledTools is documented only as "when set, the exact tools the roster reports".

  skillSettings?: {
    skills?: string[]; // omitted from a sent skillSettings, it becomes []
    tools?: { name: string; autoCall?: boolean }[];
    enabledSkills?: string[] | null; // when set, the exact Skills the roster reports
    enabledTools?: string[] | null; // when set, the exact tools the roster reports
  };
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/references/capabilities.md:467–467E3

capabilities.md: a tool that registers as GENERAL "reaches every employee whose tool selection has never been saved"; narrowing it for one employee means that employee's tool selection in AI settings or a session skillSettings allowlist.

A server's tools register as `GENERAL` tools named `mcp-<server>-<tool>`, so every employee whose tool selection has never been saved has them from the moment the server connects. Once an employee's tool switches are saved in AI settings, that selection is a fixed list, and a tool discovered afterwards — a new MCP tool included — stays off for that employee until someone switches it on. Use that exact name wherever a tool is named: in a Skill's `tools`, an employee's `tools`, or a session's `skillSettings`. Narrowing them for one employee is its tool selection in AI settings, or a session's `skillSettings` allowlist.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:277–293E10

isToolSelected makes skillSettings.enabledTools the outer boundary "including system tools"; when no selection is saved the legacy filter admits all GENERAL tools.

    isToolSelected(name) {
        const enabled = this.employee.skillSettings?.enabledTools;
        // Employee selection is the outer boundary, including system tools.
        if (Array.isArray(enabled) && !enabled.includes(name))
            return false;
        const settings = this.skillSettings;
        const filter = settings?.tools;
        if (!Array.isArray(filter))
            return true;
        if (!settings?.toolsVersion && filter.length === 0)
            return true;
        // Preserve the legacy session exception; it never bypasses employee policy.
        return ([
            ...listSystemTools(),
            LOAD_FRONTEND_TOOL_NAME,
            EXECUTE_FRONTEND_TOOL_NAME,
        ].includes(name) || filter.includes(name));
app/server/ai/index.ts:28–52E12

App comment records that defineAIEmployee cannot express enabledTools, so restrictToolsToSearch reads the employee and upserts skillSettings.enabledTools = ['search-materials'].

/**
 * Pins the assistant's tool allow-list to its one read-only tool.
 *
 * `defineAIEmployee` cannot express `enabledTools`, and the AI plugin marks
 * every built-in tool it ships — form filling, charting, data queries, web
 * search and the sub-agent orchestration tools — `GENERAL`, which makes all of
 * them eligible for any employee that does not narrow the list. The materials
 * assistant must only ever be able to search materials, so its first
 * registration records the allow-list; a later administrator selection is left
 * untouched.
 */
async function restrictToolsToSearch(
  aiEmployeeManager: AIEmployeeManager,
  username: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const employee = await aiEmployeeManager.getEmployee(username);
  if (!employee || employee.skillSettings?.enabledTools != null) return;
  await aiEmployeeManager.upsertEmployee({
    ...employee,
    skillSettings: {
      ...employee.skillSettings,
      enabledTools: ['search-materials'],
    },
  });
}

F6 · 类型化 seed 记录与 Repository 的 RepositoryRecord 约束存在索引签名摩擦

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/db · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

候选成立（usability-improvement）。Repository 默认 TRecord=RepositoryRecord（Record<string,...>），createOne 的 values 需带索引签名；interface 不具隐式索引签名，故 interface MaterialSeed 传给 createOne 报 TS2769。agent-repair-3 历史记录了该 TS2769 报错，当前种子已改用 type 别名调用 createOne。属真实类型工效/可用性缺口，建议放宽 Repository 值类型或在文档中说明。

  • 职责与公开约定：Repository<TRecord=RepositoryRecord> 默认记录类型为 Record<string,...>，createOne 的 values 经 MutationValuesInput/创建值约束，要求可赋给带索引签名的对象。 E33
  • 实际行为证据：原始材料显示 createOne({values: material})（material: interface MaterialSeed）触发 TS2769『Index signature for type string is missing in type MaterialSeed』，修复方式是把 interface 改成 type 别名以获得隐式索引签名。 E168
  • 应用接入与业务边界：App 种子文件当前已用 type MaterialSeed 别名并正常调用 findOne/createOne，说明 workaround 被采纳，属类型工效问题而非业务错误。 E35
  • 环境因素：现象只由冻结的 Repository 类型定义和 TypeScript 结构化类型规则决定，与外部服务、凭据或安装无关。 E33
  • 工厂因素：工厂 typecheck 阶段真实暴露该 TS2769，agent-repair-3 历史保留报错与修复合入过程；非工厂注入或脚本覆盖造成。 E168
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：现有泛型/内联对象可绕过，但默认 createOne 用法仍会踩坑；放宽值类型或补充文档的改进空间成立。 E33 E35

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

Repository 默认 TRecord=RepositoryRecord，createOne 的 values 经 MutationValuesInput/ScalarCreateValues 约束。以 interface 声明的业务记录没有隐式 string 索引签名，传给 createOne 触发 TS2769（Index signature for type string is missing）；改为 type alias 即通过。db SKILL 的 seed 示例只使用对象字面量，未覆盖该情形。

实际影响
以强类型业务模型写 seed/迁移时会遇到非直觉的类型错误，需改写类型定义或加断言，增加首次接入成本。
建议改法
或让写入泛型接受显式接口（放宽 index signature 约束），或在 db SKILL 的 seed 章节说明索引签名要求并给出 type alias 示例。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
用 interface MaterialSeed 数组作为 seed 数据调用 repository.createOne({ values }).
应有能力 / 行为
类型化记录可直接写入，类型检查通过。
实际观察
TypeScript 报 TS2769 / 「Index signature for type string is missing」。
应用绕行与代价
把 interface 改为 type MaterialSeed = {...}（或加索引签名/断言）。
改进后的验收标准
Repository 写入泛型接受显式接口类型，或 db SKILL 明确记录该限制并给出示例。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 2 条
packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/types.d.ts:199–246E33

Repository defaults its record type to RepositoryRecord; createOne takes CreateOneOptions<TCreate, TRecord>, which constrains the values shape through MutationValuesInput/ScalarCreateValues.

export interface Repository<TRecord extends object = RepositoryRecord, TCreate extends object = Partial<TRecord>, TUpdate extends object = Partial<TRecord>> {
    withPolicy<const TPolicy extends RepositoryPolicy<TRecord>>(policy: TPolicy): ScopedRepository<PolicyRecord<TRecord, TPolicy>, TCreate, TUpdate>;
    withPolicy<P, const TPolicy extends RepositoryPolicy<TRecord>>(policy: (principal: P) => TPolicy, principal: P): ScopedRepository<PolicyRecord<TRecord, TPolicy>, TCreate, TUpdate>;
    findMany<TSelection extends AnySelectBuilder<TRecord>>(options: FindManyOptions<TRecord> & {
        readonly select: (select: SelectBuilder<TRecord>) => TSelection;
    }): RepositoryQuery<SelectedBuilderRecord<TRecord, TSelection>>;
    findMany(options?: FindManyOptions<TRecord>): RepositoryQuery<TRecord>;
    findOne<TSelection extends AnySelectBuilder<TRecord>>(options: FindOneOptions<TRecord> & {
        readonly select: (select: SelectBuilder<TRecord>) => TSelection;
    }): Promise<SelectedBuilderRecord<TRecord, TSelection> | undefined>;
    findOne(options: FindOneOptions<TRecord>): Promise<TRecord | undefined>;
    count(options?: FilterOnlyOptions<TRecord>): Promise<number>;
    exists(options?: FilterOnlyOptions<TRecord>): Promise<boolean>;
    aggregate<TSelection extends AggregateSelection>(options: AggregateOptions<TRecord> & {
        readonly aggregate: (aggregate: AggregateBuilder<TRecord>) => TSelection;
    }): Promise<AggregateSelectionResult<TSelection>>;
    aggregate(options: AggregateOptions<TRecord> & {
        readonly aggregate: AggregateAst;
    }): Promise<AggregateResult>;
    aggregate(options: AggregateOptions<TRecord>): Promise<AggregateResult>;
    groupBy<const TBy extends readonly [
        keyof TRecord & string,
        ...(keyof TRecord & string)[]
    ], TSelection extends AggregateSelection>(options: GroupByOptions<TRecord, GroupBySelectionResult<TRecord, TBy, TSelection>> & {
        readonly by: TBy;
        readonly aggregate: (aggregate: AggregateBuilder<TRecord>) => TSelection;
    }): Promise<Array<GroupBySelectionResult<TRecord, TBy, TSelection>>>;
    groupBy(options: GroupByOptions<TRecord> & {
        readonly aggregate: AggregateAst;
    }): Promise<GroupByResult[]>;
    groupBy(options: GroupByOptions<TRecord>): Promise<GroupByResult[]>;
    describeMutation(options: DescribeMutationOptions): Promise<RepositoryMutationDescription>;
    validateMutation(options: ValidateMutationOptions<TCreate, TUpdate, TRecord>): Promise<MutationValidationResult>;
    createOne<TSelection extends AnySelectBuilder<TRecord>>(options: CreateOneOptions<TCreate, TRecord> & {
        readonly select: (select: SelectBuilder<TRecord>) => TSelection;
    }): Promise<SingleMutationResult<SelectedBuilderRecord<TRecord, TSelection>>>;
    createOne(options: CreateOneOptions<TCreate, TRecord>): Promise<SingleMutationResult<TRecord>>;
    createMany<TSelection extends AnySelectBuilder<TRecord>>(options: CreateManyOptions<TCreate, TRecord> & {
        readonly select: (select: SelectBuilder<TRecord>) => TSelection;
    }): Promise<CreateManyResult<SelectedBuilderRecord<TRecord, TSelection>>>;
    createMany(options: CreateManyOptions<TCreate, TRecord> & {
        readonly select: SelectAst;
    }): Promise<CreateManyResult<TRecord>>;
    createMany(options: CreateManyOptions<TCreate, TRecord>): Promise<CreateManyResult>;
    updateOne<TSelection extends AnySelectBuilder<TRecord>>(options: UpdateOneOptions<TUpdate, TRecord> & {
        readonly select: (select: SelectBuilder<TRecord>) => TSelection;
    }): Promise<SingleMutationResult<SelectedBuilderRecord<TRecord, TSelection>>>;
    updateOne(options: UpdateOneOptions<TUpdate, TRecord>): Promise<SingleMutationResult<TRecord>>;
app/database/main/seeds/202610010002_seed_materials.ts:35–50E35

App seed uses defineSeed + context.repository; it does findOne + createOne so a repeat run inserts only missing titles and never overwrites a supervisor edit.

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202610010002_seed_materials',

  async run(context) {
    const materialsRepository = context.repository('materials');

    for (const material of materials) {
      const existing = await materialsRepository.findOne({
        filter: { title: material.title },
      });

      if (!existing) {
        await materialsRepository.createOne({ values: material });
      }
    }
  },
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][易用性改进] 类型化 seed 记录与 Repository 的 RepositoryRecord 约束存在索引签名摩擦

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.17）

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

候选成立（usability-improvement）。Repository 默认 TRecord=RepositoryRecord（Record<string,...>），createOne 的 values 需带索引签名；interface 不具隐式索引签名，故 interface MaterialSeed 传给 createOne 报 TS2769。agent-repair-3 历史记录了该 TS2769 报错，当前种子已改用 type 别名调用 createOne。属真实类型工效/可用性缺口，建议放宽 Repository 值类型或在文档中说明。

- 职责与公开约定：Repository<TRecord=RepositoryRecord> 默认记录类型为 Record<string,...>，createOne 的 values 经 MutationValuesInput/创建值约束，要求可赋给带索引签名的对象。；证据：E33（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/types.d.ts:199–246）
- 实际行为证据：原始材料显示 createOne({values: material})（material: interface MaterialSeed）触发 TS2769『Index signature for type string is missing in type MaterialSeed』，修复方式是把 interface 改成 type 别名以获得隐式索引签名。；证据：E168（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-3.jsonl/part-0001.txt:2556–2556）
- 应用接入与业务边界：App 种子文件当前已用 type MaterialSeed 别名并正常调用 findOne/createOne，说明 workaround 被采纳，属类型工效问题而非业务错误。；证据：E35（app/database/main/seeds/202610010002_seed_materials.ts:35–50）
- 环境因素：现象只由冻结的 Repository 类型定义和 TypeScript 结构化类型规则决定，与外部服务、凭据或安装无关。；证据：E33（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/types.d.ts:199–246）
- 工厂因素：工厂 typecheck 阶段真实暴露该 TS2769，agent-repair-3 历史保留报错与修复合入过程；非工厂注入或脚本覆盖造成。；证据：E168（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-3.jsonl/part-0001.txt:2556–2556）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：现有泛型/内联对象可绕过，但默认 createOne 用法仍会踩坑；放宽值类型或补充文档的改进空间成立。；证据：E33（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/types.d.ts:199–246）、E35（app/database/main/seeds/202610010002_seed_materials.ts:35–50）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

Repository 默认 TRecord=RepositoryRecord，createOne 的 values 经 MutationValuesInput/ScalarCreateValues 约束。以 interface 声明的业务记录没有隐式 string 索引签名，传给 createOne 触发 TS2769（Index signature for type string is missing）；改为 type alias 即通过。db SKILL 的 seed 示例只使用对象字面量，未覆盖该情形。

**影响**：以强类型业务模型写 seed/迁移时会遇到非直觉的类型错误，需改写类型定义或加断言，增加首次接入成本。

**建议改动**：或让写入泛型接受显式接口（放宽 index signature 约束），或在 db SKILL 的 seed 章节说明索引签名要求并给出 type alias 示例。

**验收标准**：Repository 写入泛型接受显式接口类型，或 db SKILL 明确记录该限制并给出示例。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/types.d.ts:199–246` — Repository defaults its record type to RepositoryRecord; createOne takes CreateOneOptions<TCreate, TRecord>, which constrains the values shape through MutationValuesInput/ScalarCreateValues.
- `app/database/main/seeds/202610010002_seed_materials.ts:35–50` — App seed uses defineSeed + context.repository; it does findOne + createOne so a repeat run inserts only missing titles and never overwrites a supervisor edit.

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 37859683993 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
实现者过程与运行背景 · 5 个过程问题 / 1 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

Built the missing materials assistant feature and made the browser-stage acceptance pass end to end. Completed the server model (materials collection, composite authorization resource, seeded permission sets and isolated accounts, read-only search tool and employee), the app-owned page with URL-addressable overlays and gated create/edit/delete, the legacy /api/materials:list and :get aliases the QA probed, and an integration test that boots the real standalone server and asserts the whole access model. In this round fixed the new integration test's 403 (cookie-write CSRF in a bare test server) and a real client bug where a route param id (text) was sent as a Repository filter for an integer column.

问题 01 · lint

unbound-method on destructured seed repository

本任务已处理

pnpm lint failed: database/main/seeds/202610010002_seed_materials.ts 38:15 error @typescript-eslint/unbound-method — a method not declared with `this: void` may cause unintentional scoping of `this` when separated from its object.

原因（实现者自述）
The seed callback destructured `repository` from the SeedContext parameter (`async run({ repository })`). SeedContext.repository is declared as a method, so referencing it detached from its object trips the unbound-method rule.
处理方式
Receive the whole context (`async run(context)`) and call `context.repository('materials')`, keeping the method bound to its object. Runtime behavior is unchanged.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：database/main/seeds/202610010002_seed_materials.ts
问题 02 · typecheck

MaterialSeed not assignable to RepositoryRecord

本任务已处理

tsconfig.server.json typecheck (the stage after lint) reported TS2769 on createOne({ values: material }) — Index signature for type 'string' is missing in type 'MaterialSeed'.

原因（实现者自述）
MaterialSeed was declared as an interface, which does not receive an implicit index signature, while Repository.createOne expects values assignable to RepositoryRecord (Record<string, RepositoryMutationScalarValue>).
处理方式
Declared MaterialSeed as an object type alias, which TypeScript treats as compatible with the record index signature. The seed data and behavior are unchanged.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：database/main/seeds/202610010002_seed_materials.ts
问题 03 · test

Supervisor findMany answered 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN

本任务已处理

The new materials integration test signed in with a cookie and POSTed /api/materials/findMany; the server answered 403 PERMISSION_DENIED reason INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN. Permission sets and assignments were correct, so the failure looked like an authorization bug.

原因（实现者自述）
The authentication plugin rejects a cookie-authenticated write whose Origin is neither the application's own nor a trusted one (auth.js checkCookieWriteOrigin). A bare standalone test server has no configured trusted origin, so a cookie POST is refused before authorization runs. It was never a permission problem.
处理方式
The integration test now signs in with a cookie and then creates an API key for each account and sends it as x-api-key. An API key is not an ambient browser credential, so the CSRF check returns early, and the writes exercise the same path the verifier uses. The browser client sends Origin for POST, so production is unaffected.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：tests/logic/app-server.test.ts
问题 04 · runtime

Route-param id (text) sent as an integer Repository filter

本任务已处理

GET /api/materials:get?id=3 answered 400 RepositoryError: Filter value is invalid for Field "id" of type "integer". The client detail drawer and edit dialog built findOne({ filter: { id: materialId } }) from a useParams string, which would have failed the same way at runtime.

原因（实现者自述）
Route params and query strings are strings, while the materials id column is an integer and the Repository validates a filter value against the column type. The handler passed the raw string through.
处理方式
Added toMaterialId() in client/pages/materials/types.ts and coerceSelectorValue() in server/materials/read.ts. The client converts the param to a number before the read and treats an unusable param as "not found" without asking the server; the :get route coerces a numeric query string. Material.id is now typed number.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：client/pages/materials/types.ts, server/materials/read.ts
问题 05 · runtime

The assistant could have called write-capable built-in tools

本任务已处理

The requirement is that the assistant only reads materials — it must not create or modify anything, send messages, search the web or delegate to other agents. The AI plugin ships nineteen built-in tools (form filling, charting, data queries, web search, sub-agent orchestration) marked `GENERAL`, so by default every one of them is eligible for a newly registered employee.

原因（实现者自述）
The employee was registered with `tools: [{ name: 'search-materials', autoCall: true }]`, but `tools` only adds a specific tool; it does not remove the `GENERAL` set. Eligibility is governed by `skillSettings.enabledTools`, and `defineAIEmployee` has no option for it.
处理方式
After registration the registrar records `skillSettings.enabledTools: ['search-materials']` when the employee has no selection yet, so only the one read-only tool is eligible. A later administrator selection is preserved, the chat window additionally passes `enableWebSearch={false}` and `enableAttachments={false}`, and an integration assertion pins `enabledTools` to `['search-materials']`.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：server/ai/index.ts

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · ai-employee-contract

defineAIEmployee cannot express a tool allow-list

需人工判断

The AI plugin marks every built-in tool it ships (form filling, charting, data queries, web search, sub-agent orchestration) `GENERAL`, which makes all of them eligible for any employee that does not narrow the list. `defineAIEmployee` exposes `tools` (name + autoCall) but no `enabledTools`, and eligibility is read only from the registered employee's `skillSettings.enabledTools`. An application that wants a strictly read-only employee therefore cannot express it at registration time.

建议改动

Let `AIEmployeeOptions` carry `enabledTools`/`enabledSkills` so `toBuiltInEmployee` seeds them on first registration, and document that `tools` alone does not restrict the general tool set.

来源：Agent · retro.json

查看原始复盘数据

独立框架评审：以「只读资料 + AI 助手」为例检验 NocoBase3 的 AI 员工、授权、应用装配与 DB 四个能力单元。总体判断是框架能力强、指引密集：授权组合、typed Repository 路由、ServiceProvider 装配、migration/seed 与 URL 可寻址浮层均由公开 API 与详尽指引支撑，App 未修改插件内部即完成实现。唯一实质框架侧缺口是 AI 员工的注册期工具边界：AIEmployeeOptions 无 enabledTools/enabledSkills，运行时外边界 enabledTools 只能在注册后由 upsertEmployee 写入，且未保存选择时默认放行全部 GENERAL 内置工具；Employee Skill 的完成检查「工具列表正好显示所声明的」与 api-reference 对 enabledTools 的「roster 报告」措辞会掩盖这一点。136 条过程错误信号全部为探索性命令非零退出、环境/超时、验证产物缺失或工具约束，未发现由框架导致的运行时失败。

AI 员工与工具边界（@nocobase/ai-employee / app-plugin-ai-employee）对象、需求覆盖、五项理由与证据

App 需注册一个只读资料助手：声明 App 工具、注册员工并保证该员工只能调用这一个只读工具。库侧提供 defineTools/defineAIEmployee/AIEmployeeManager；插件侧在运行时装配员工上下文与候选工具集并以 skillSettings.enabledTools 过滤。本单元评判该库/插件/指引是否把「注册即边界」做成可用能力。

框架职责与业务边界

推荐使用方式

E12 E13

框架职责与业务边界
框架负责注册期工具边界；这是框架库的输入契约问题，不是业务规则。
推荐使用方式
指引未给出注册期 allow-list 写法；api-reference 只把 enabledTools 描述为「roster 报告的精确工具」。
Agent 实际使用
App 先 registerEmployee，再 getEmployee 后调用 upsertEmployee 写入 skillSettings.enabledTools=['search-materials']，并附注释说明 defineAIEmployee 无法表达。
缺口归因
NocoBase3 内核
E7 E8 E9 E10 E3 E12

框架职责与业务边界
框架运行时。
推荐使用方式
enabledTools 为外边界（含系统工具）；选择不等于能力授予，仍需 actor 权限与 KB 配置。
Agent 实际使用
context.js isToolSelected 先按 enabledTools 过滤，再叠加系统工具白名单、webSearch 与 knowledge-base 检查。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

授权模型（@nocobase/authorization / app-plugin-authorization）对象、需求覆盖、五项理由与证据

资料必须做记录级读过滤：普通同事只能读非保密行，主管可读全部；写操作只允许改 title/body。App 通过 composite resource、database permission、record access 与 authorizeRepository/withPolicy 组合实现，并在 AI 工具中复用同一策略。

首轮与修复过程 · 查看逐轮原始状态
轮次范围原始结论失败项
验证 2全量浏览器 QA报告未通过B00 不依赖 AI 服务即可验收：主管维护三条资料，普通同事只能阅读 A/B，C 的页面及直接读取接口均拒绝普通同事访问；刷新后资料仍保存。未配置模型时助手显示明确未配置状态，资料页仍可用。 以下 B01–B05 在独立业务测试模型配置可用后执行；缺模型时逐项记 blocked，继续完成资料、权限、助手接入和未配置状态的搭建，不用固定回复替代真实调用。
B01 普通同事询问报修电话，回答与资料 A 一致，能从引用打开有权阅读的原资料。
B02 普通同事询问保密项目代号，回答、引用和错误提示均不包含资料 C 的保密内容；也不能通过引用链接打开 C。
B03 刷新页面，刚才的对话仍能恢复并继续提问。
B04 主管将资料 B 的巡检间隔改为 60 天，普通同事在新对话里重新询问，依据更新后的资料回答 60 天，而非旧的 45 天。
B05 询问资料中没有的“设备免费更换年限”，助手说明缺少依据，不编造年限。
验证 4失败项复测（非全量）报告未通过B00 不依赖 AI 服务即可验收：主管维护三条资料，普通同事只能阅读 A/B，C 的页面及直接读取接口均拒绝普通同事访问；刷新后资料仍保存。未配置模型时助手显示明确未配置状态，资料页仍可用。 以下 B01–B05 在独立业务测试模型配置可用后执行；缺模型时逐项记 blocked，继续完成资料、权限、助手接入和未配置状态的搭建，不用固定回复替代真实调用。

authorization 以 defineCompositeResource/defineRecordAccess 声明业务动作与可复用记录选择，app-plugin-authorization 的 defineDatabasePermission/authorizeRepository/withPolicy 把决策下沉到 Repository。App 用同一 composite view 决策同时服务页面读取与 search-materials 工具，未出现「先读后过滤」的绕过路径。

提供的帮助
保密资料在数据库层即被过滤，AI 与页面行为一致，降低了权限旁路风险。
应保留的能力
保持；可在文档中把「AI 工具复用同一 policy」明确为一个推荐模式。

问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 冻结快照评审：未核对上游最新版本；confirmed 仅表示快照材料中有直接证据。
  • 未安装依赖、未运行应用，也未调用外部服务；未独立复现 QA 判定。
  • AI 链路因缺少可用测试模型，B01–B05 为 blocked、B06 为 not_run；AI 行为结论来自源码与 App 使用证据。
  • 仅审查与任务直接相关的 4 个能力单元，未逐字通读全部框架源码。
  • historyReview 依据归档 JSONL 事件做错误信号处置，不证明模型已通读或理解每一字节；过程错误信号不等于框架缺陷。
  • 仅凭本单例不推断整个框架的普遍行为。
  • 快照未包含 5 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。

E1 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/SKILL.md:90–98

Completion checks for an App-defined AI employee. Line 94 asserts that after start "the employee's tool list shows exactly what was declared".

## Completion checks

- A first-time visitor lands on the page, sees a usable composer without switching employee or model, sends, and gets a reply. Reload and repeat: this tests a fresh mount, not a warm one.
- Each unhappy configuration shows its own actionable message rather than a composer that looks ready: discovery loading, discovery failed, no accessible employees, model discovery failed, no enabled models.
- The App employee, tool, and skill appear in AI settings after start, and the employee's tool list shows exactly what was declared.
- The new tool runs from chat, is approved when its permission is `ASK`, returns a serializable result, and leaves the expected database row behind. Run it twice and verify no duplicate.
- An unauthorized user is refused by the tool, not only by the prompt.
- The employee the page opens on is the one intended, not whichever sorts first.
- If the chat opens from a floating trigger: clicking the trigger opens the dialog or side panel, and closing it brings the trigger back. A surface that stays shut is holding its own `open` state instead of the controller's; see [chat-surfaces.md § Surfaces](references/chat-surfaces.md#surfaces).

原文件 SHA-256：4a1f05d2097655629a6ebab6d47b8099ad5f772b8c4b1a18803ced67eb4f9013

E2 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/references/capabilities.md:78–81

capabilities.md states the persisted employee record carries enabled/builtIn/deprecated/about/defaultPrompt/skillSettings/knowledgeBase/model settings, which are "administered in AI settings. Do not put them in the definition."

`skills` and `tools` are the employee's declared capability set, but listing a tool is not always enough to reach it: a tool named by any registered Skill stays behind that Skill until the conversation loads it. Read [How Skills and Tools relate](#how-skills-and-tools-relate) before deciding which of the two lists a capability belongs in — the choice is not cosmetic, and the failure mode is a tool that never activates and never complains.

The persisted employee record carries more than the definition does — `enabled`, `builtIn`, `deprecated`, `about`, `defaultPrompt`, `skillSettings`, `knowledgeBase`, model settings. Those are administered in AI settings. Do not put them in the definition.

原文件 SHA-256：19952d42c463b0c7c62774848e82aa0e5aa53ad3b87a7e6b57a7d488f683d8ed

E3 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/references/capabilities.md:467–467

capabilities.md: a tool that registers as GENERAL "reaches every employee whose tool selection has never been saved"; narrowing it for one employee means that employee's tool selection in AI settings or a session skillSettings allowlist.

A server's tools register as `GENERAL` tools named `mcp-<server>-<tool>`, so every employee whose tool selection has never been saved has them from the moment the server connects. Once an employee's tool switches are saved in AI settings, that selection is a fixed list, and a tool discovered afterwards — a new MCP tool included — stays off for that employee until someone switches it on. Use that exact name wherever a tool is named: in a Skill's `tools`, an employee's `tools`, or a session's `skillSettings`. Narrowing them for one employee is its tool selection in AI settings, or a session's `skillSettings` allowlist.

原文件 SHA-256：19952d42c463b0c7c62774848e82aa0e5aa53ad3b87a7e6b57a7d488f683d8ed

E6 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-ai-employee/references/chat-surfaces.md:22–30

chat-surfaces.md: the React UI is the shadcn Registry item nocobase-ai, installed as App source at client/extensions/nocobase-ai; the plugin package exports no chat UI.

## Install the extension

The React UI is a shadcn Registry item named `nocobase-ai`, owned by `@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee` and installed as App source at `client/extensions/nocobase-ai`. It is App-owned once installed: edit and commit it like any other App file. The plugin package exports no chat UI, so a missing extension is never a reason to import React components from `@nocobase/ai-employee`, to patch the plugin, or to rebuild chat under `client/`.

Check for `client/extensions/nocobase-ai/index.ts` first. If it exists, reuse it. If not, use the first option below that applies.

### Option 1 — from the App's own installed plugin (preferred)

The published plugin ships its canonical source and a self-contained Registry JSON, so a generated App can install from the copy already resolved in its own `node_modules`. The installed UI then matches the plugin version the App actually runs, with no network access and no NocoBase source checkout. Run from the App root:

E8 · packages/@nocobase/ai-employee/dist/manager/ai-employee/default.js:80–104

registerEmployeeInRepository builds the stored record from toBuiltInEmployee; enabledSkills/enabledTools are only carried over from an existing row or a sourceSettings argument that the public registerEmployee never passes.

    async registerEmployeeInRepository(repository, options, sourceSettings) {
        const current = (await repository.findOne({
            filter: { username: options.username },
        })) ?? undefined;
        const value = this.toBuiltInEmployee(options, current);
        // Registration refreshes legacy arrays, never an administrator's override.
        for (const key of ['enabledSkills', 'enabledTools']) {
            const selection = current?.skillSettings?.[key] !== undefined
                ? current.skillSettings[key]
                : sourceSettings?.[key];
            if (selection !== undefined) {
                value.skillSettings[key] =
                    selection === null ? null : [...new Set(selection)];
            }
        }
        if (Array.isArray(value.skillSettings.enabledTools)) {
            // The manager cannot classify dynamic tools by scope. Keep all saved
            // settings; runtime only consults these permissions for CUSTOM tools.
            // Saved ASK must win over a subsequently registered ALLOW default.
            value.skillSettings.tools = [
                ...new Map([
                    ...value.skillSettings.tools,
                    ...(sourceSettings?.tools ?? []),
                    ...(current?.skillSettings?.tools ?? []),
                ].map((tool) => [tool.name, tool])).values(),

E9 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:224–260

getAIEmployeeTools starts from every GENERAL tool plus getSkill/web search, then merges skillSettings.tools and skillSettings.enabledTools into the candidate set.

    async getAIEmployeeTools(discoveredToolMap) {
        if (this.chatSettings.enableTools === false)
            return [];
        const currentFrontendTools = await listCurrentFrontendTools(this.conversations, { sessionId: this.sessionId, frontendTools: this.frontendTools });
        const tools = await this.listTools({ scope: 'GENERAL' });
        const getSkill = await this.toolsManager.getTools(SYSTEM_TOOLS.GET_SKILL, {
            ctx: this.agentContext,
        });
        if (getSkill)
            tools.push(getSkill);
        if (this.webSearch === true) {
            const webSearch = await this.toolsManager.getTools(SYSTEM_TOOLS.WEB_SEARCH, {
                ctx: this.agentContext,
            });
            if (webSearch)
                tools.push(webSearch);
        }
        const generalNames = new Set(tools.map((tool) => tool.definition.name));
        const toolMap = discoveredToolMap ?? (await this.listToolMap());
        const configured = [
            ...(this.employee.skillSettings?.tools ?? []),
            ...(this.employee.skillSettings?.enabledTools ?? []).map((name) => ({
                name,
            })),
        ];
        if (await this.getKnowledgeBaseRetrieveTool())
            configured.push({ name: SYSTEM_TOOLS.KNOWLEDGE_BASE });
        for (const setting of configured) {
            if (!generalNames.has(setting.name)) {
                const tool = toolMap.get(setting.name);
                if (tool)
                    tools.push(tool);
            }
        }
        const eligible = await this.getEligibleToolNames(tools.map((tool) => tool.definition.name));
        return prepareToolsForFrontendConversation(tools.filter((tool) => eligible.has(tool.definition.name)), currentFrontendTools);
    }

E10 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee/dist/server/agent/context/ai-employee/context.js:277–293

isToolSelected makes skillSettings.enabledTools the outer boundary "including system tools"; when no selection is saved the legacy filter admits all GENERAL tools.

    isToolSelected(name) {
        const enabled = this.employee.skillSettings?.enabledTools;
        // Employee selection is the outer boundary, including system tools.
        if (Array.isArray(enabled) && !enabled.includes(name))
            return false;
        const settings = this.skillSettings;
        const filter = settings?.tools;
        if (!Array.isArray(filter))
            return true;
        if (!settings?.toolsVersion && filter.length === 0)
            return true;
        // Preserve the legacy session exception; it never bypasses employee policy.
        return ([
            ...listSystemTools(),
            LOAD_FRONTEND_TOOL_NAME,
            EXECUTE_FRONTEND_TOOL_NAME,
        ].includes(name) || filter.includes(name));

E12 · app/server/ai/index.ts:28–52

App comment records that defineAIEmployee cannot express enabledTools, so restrictToolsToSearch reads the employee and upserts skillSettings.enabledTools = ['search-materials'].

/**
 * Pins the assistant's tool allow-list to its one read-only tool.
 *
 * `defineAIEmployee` cannot express `enabledTools`, and the AI plugin marks
 * every built-in tool it ships — form filling, charting, data queries, web
 * search and the sub-agent orchestration tools — `GENERAL`, which makes all of
 * them eligible for any employee that does not narrow the list. The materials
 * assistant must only ever be able to search materials, so its first
 * registration records the allow-list; a later administrator selection is left
 * untouched.
 */
async function restrictToolsToSearch(
  aiEmployeeManager: AIEmployeeManager,
  username: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const employee = await aiEmployeeManager.getEmployee(username);
  if (!employee || employee.skillSettings?.enabledTools != null) return;
  await aiEmployeeManager.upsertEmployee({
    ...employee,
    skillSettings: {
      ...employee.skillSettings,
      enabledTools: ['search-materials'],
    },
  });
}

原文件 SHA-256：0e3f2223bdc5b57335bb69f0e37e4c3065c7e7a4f572942bc0f9b6bc3d60122e

E13 · app/client/pages/materials/materials-assistant.tsx:42–79

The App chat surface gates on configurationStatus/employees/hasEnabledModels and mounts the chat only once a usable employee and model are observed.

function ConfiguredMaterialsAssistant(): ReactElement {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const {
    configurationStatus,
    configurationError,
    modelConfigurationError,
    employees,
    hasEnabledModels,
  } = useAI();

  if (configurationStatus === 'loading') {
    return (
      <p className='text-sm text-muted-foreground' role='status'>
        {t('materials.assistant.loading')}
      </p>
    );
  }

  if (configurationStatus === 'error') {
    return (
      <AssistantNotice
        title={t('materials.assistant.unavailable.title')}
        description={
          configurationError?.message ??
          t('materials.assistant.unavailable.description')
        }
      />
    );
  }

        title={t('materials.assistant.unavailable.title')}
        description={t('materials.assistant.noEmployee')}
      />
    );
  }

E14 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:55–63

The authorization Skill prescribes the implementation order: defineDatabasePermission, defineCompositeResource, register record access/composites in the provider boot, and bind routes with authorizeRepository or withPolicy.

## Implement in this order

1. Declare schema and relationships through self-contained migrations. Choose stable composite, page and data scope names. Keep authorization decisions out of migration code.
2. Declare reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission`; compose user-facing business operations as composite resources with `defineCompositeResource` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`. Explicitly name fields, relation capabilities and every collection a workflow touches.
3. In the owning provider's `boot`, opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add`, register composites with `authz.compositeResources.define`, record access with `authz.recordAccess.define` and settings items with `authz.settings.add`. Then add a workspace subsection with `authz.ui.sections.add({ name, title, parent })` and list each composite or settings item in it with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section, group? })` (the permission workspace lists sections and subsections on the left and the selected subsection's resources, under optional `authz.ui.groups`, on the right; an unplaced composite or settings item goes under its default section's Other with a startup warning, and a misplaced one fails a development start). Pages are not registered on the server: the client route tree lists them. Release subject registrations at shutdown.
4. Protect every server route with authentication. For a simple single-scope Repository operation, keep `defineRepositoryApiRoutes` and add `authz.database.authorizeRepository` to map methods to composite actions; it reuses or initializes the request's authorization context. For workflows, persisted-state checks, multiple data scopes or custom responses, install `authz.middleware()`, call `c.get('authz').authorize()` once and bind every returned collection policy with `repository.withPolicy()`. Follow [Repository route integration](references/repository-routes.md).
5. Declare `authz` on every entry client route (nested pages inherit it; an omitted one defaults to root-only or `'skip'` with a development warning), add `useCan` for action visibility and obtain per-record eligibility from the server when needed. Handle pending and error states without displaying stale access.
6. Complete the accompanying permission configuration from the responsibility matrix: sets, page and action grants, selected data scopes, supported rules and intended assignments. Use seeds for fresh installations and authorized provisioning for existing installations; editing a seed does not update a running App. Resolve optional capabilities through their installed Skills and preserve unrelated administrator edits.
7. Verify denied as well as allowed requests through the production route factory and actual database policies. Then verify the UI with ordinary users and inherited memberships.

E19 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/authorizer.js:27–45

DatabaseResourceAuthorizer.authorize resolves the collection and the grants/constraints for the request; unknown collections/fields are denied rather than silently allowed.

    async authorize(request, grantsService, constraintsService) {
        const resourceId = request.resource.id;
        const resolved = await this.collection(resourceId, request.action);
        if ('effect' in resolved)
            return resolved;
        const resource = resolved;
        const params = request.params;
        if (!databaseCollectionFieldsKnown(resource, params?.fields)) {
            return this.deny('UNKNOWN_DATABASE_FIELD', `One or more requested fields are not registered for ${resourceId}`);
        }
        const resolvedGrants = await grantsService.resolve({
            principal: request.principal,
            subjects: request.subjects,
            resource: { type: 'database.collection', id: resourceId },
            action: request.action,
        });
        const grants = params?.operation
            ? resolvedGrants.filter((grant) => grant.origin?.resource.id === params.operation?.resource &&
                grant.origin?.action === params.operation?.action)

E22 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md:1–50

server-routes.md: endpoints live in server/routes/ and are exported as an array; every /api route carries describeRoute() and validates input with apiValidator().

# Server routes

HTTP endpoints live in `server/routes/` and are listed in the array `server/routes/index.ts` exports, which `server/runtime.ts` passes to the application.

## Two kinds

| Function             | Path in code     | Final URL        | For                                              |
| -------------------- | ---------------- | ---------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `defineApiRoutes()`  | `/orders`        | `/api/orders`    | Application APIs the browser calls               |
| `defineRootRoutes()` | `/callbacks/pay` | `/callbacks/pay` | Webhooks, OAuth callbacks, protocol entry points |

Do not repeat `/api` in the path, and never write the deployment base path such as `/main` — both are added by the runtime.

Name paths, shape responses and errors, validate input and declare each route for the API document as [HTTP API design](http-api.md) describes. Every `/api` route carries a `describeRoute()` and validates its input with `apiValidator()`, both from `@nocobase/app-server/router`; a route without a declaration is a defect.

## An authenticated endpoint

```ts
import { authenticationToken } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication';
import type { Application } from '@nocobase/app-server/application';
import {
  apiErrorResponse,
  apiValidator,
  defineApiRoutes,
  describeRoute,
  listResponse,
  type AppApiRouteContribution,
} from '@nocobase/app-server/router';
import { Hono } from 'hono';

import { ListOrdersQuery, OrderSchema } from './schemas.js';

export const apiRoutes: AppApiRouteContribution<Application> = defineApiRoutes(
  (app) => {
    const router = new Hono();
    const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);
    const orders = app.container.resolve(orderServiceToken);

    router.use('/orders', auth.required());
    router.get(
      '/orders',
      describeRoute({
        tags: ['Orders'],
        summary: 'List orders',
        operationId: 'listOrders',
        responses: {
          '200': listResponse(OrderSchema),
          // It requires a session but checks no permission, so it lists its statuses instead of spreading apiErrorResponses.
          // The query validator adds the 400 for invalid input.
          '401': apiErrorResponse(401),

E23 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/overlay.md:8–16

overlay.md: create/edit short forms use RouteDialog child routes, record detail uses RouteDrawer child routes, edited as child routes by default so a link opens them and refresh restores them.

| -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------- |
| Create, edit (short form)                                                  | `RouteDialog` (child route)    | URL                        |
| Record detail view, inspector                                              | `RouteDrawer` (child route)    | URL                        |
| Child page that covers the content area (non-modal)                        | `RouteChildPage` (child route) | URL                        |
| Confirm one action (delete, deactivate, etc.)                              | `AlertDialog`                  | Component state            |
| Temporary panel that represents no record or location (explanations, help) | `Sheet`                        | Component state            |

- **Build dialogs and drawers that represent a page, form, editor or detail view as child routes by default**, even when the user does not mention "routing": a link opens them directly, a refresh restores them, and browser back and forward work. Follow an explicit user request for a different interaction.
- For these overlays, do not use an `open` state inside the component, do not add an `open` prop to `RouteDialog`/`RouteDrawer`, and do not build a separate route-overlay implementation. Route matching decides whether the overlay exists: the child route's URL opens it, and the parent route's URL closes it.

E25 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/repository-routes.d.ts:62–89

defineRepositoryApiRoutes exposes configured Repository endpoints at POST /{name}/{action} under /api, with a literal name+action path and an application-supplied principal.

export interface DefineRepositoryApiRoutesOptions<P = unknown> {
    readonly repositories: readonly RepositoryApiExposure<P>[];
    /**
     * Resolve the principal a Policy function receives. Required as soon as one
     * exposure declares a Policy function, and never called otherwise.
     *
     * The application owns this: `app-server` does not know how a request is
     * authenticated. Returning `undefined` or `null` refuses the request with
     * 403 rather than binding a Policy built from a principal that is not there,
     * which is why the return type admits both.
     */
    readonly principal?: (context: Context) => P | undefined | null | Promise<P | undefined | null>;
}
export interface RepositoryApiRoutesApplication {
    readonly container: ServiceContainer;
}
/**
 * Exposes only configured Repository endpoints using `POST /{name}/{action}`,
 * mounted under `/api`. Every path is literal — a name and an action, no
 * parameters — so the endpoints of one exposure cannot shadow each other. An
 * exposure name shares the first segment with plugin namespaces, so it must not
 * equal one: name an exposure after its Collection, such as `salesOrders`,
 * rather than after a plugin.
 *
 * This basic adapter does not install authentication or authorization. Database
 * services are resolved only when the application creates the router.
 */
export declare function defineRepositoryApiRoutes<P = unknown>(options: DefineRepositoryApiRoutesOptions<P>): AppApiRouteContribution<RepositoryApiRoutesApplication>;

E30 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:78–135

The db Skill documents migrations (defineMigration, builder.createCollection) and seeds (defineSeed, context.repository); a seed must be idempotent keyed on a unique business value.

## 2. Migrations and seeds

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job, and a seed is never sample or demonstration content.

```ts
// database/main/migrations/202609020001_create_orders.ts
import { defineMigration, type MigrationDefinition } from '@nocobase/db';

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202609020001_create_orders',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('orders', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('orderNo', { length: 64, nullable: false });
      collection.string('status', { length: 32, nullable: false });
      collection.datetime('createdAt', { nullable: false });
      collection.unique('orderNo');
      collection.index('status');
    });
  },

  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('orders');
  },
});

export default migration;
```

```ts
// database/main/seeds/202609030002_default_order_statuses.ts
import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202609030002_default_order_statuses',

  async run({ repository }) {
    await repository('orderStatuses').upsertOne({
      filter: { code: 'draft' },
      create: { code: 'draft', title: 'Draft' },
      update: { title: 'Draft' },
    });
  },
});
```

Apply both with `pnpm nocobase db apply`, adding `--connection <name>` for a non-default connection or `--all` for every managed one.

Each context carries its own tools, and which one to use is not a free choice — see [Repository and Query](#4-repository-and-query). In short: a migration works through `builder` and `query`, and a seed writes through `repository`.

The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

E35 · app/database/main/seeds/202610010002_seed_materials.ts:35–50

App seed uses defineSeed + context.repository; it does findOne + createOne so a repeat run inserts only missing titles and never overwrites a supervisor edit.

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202610010002_seed_materials',

  async run(context) {
    const materialsRepository = context.repository('materials');

    for (const material of materials) {
      const existing = await materialsRepository.findOne({
        filter: { title: material.title },
      });

      if (!existing) {
        await materialsRepository.createOne({ values: material });
      }

E36 · app/database/main/migrations/202610010001_create_materials.ts:15–36

App migration uses defineMigration + builder.createCollection with increments/string/text/boolean and a unique title.

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202610010001_create_materials',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('materials', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('title', { length: 200, nullable: false });
      collection.text('body', { nullable: false });
      collection.boolean('confidential', {
        nullable: false,
        defaultValue: false,
      });
      // Titles are the stable business key the seed and the assistant cite, so
      // they must be unique.
      collection.unique('title');
    });
  },

  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('materials');
  },
});

原始验收要求

不依赖 AI 服务即可验收：主管维护三条资料，普通同事只能阅读 A/B，C 的页面及直接读取接口均拒绝普通同事访问；刷新后资料仍保存。未配置模型时助手显示明确未配置状态，资料页仍可用。 以下 B01–B05 在独立业务测试模型配置可用后执行；缺模型时逐项记 blocked，继续完成资料、权限、助手接入和未配置状态的搭建，不用固定回复替代真实调用。

实际操作

  1. 以超级管理员 nocobase 登录，打开 /main/materials，看到 A/B/C 三条资料及助手“未配置模型”状态
  2. 点击 New material 新建“QA 临时资料”，保存成功后列表出现该资料（提示 The material was created.）
  3. 点击该资料 Edit，确认编辑弹窗回填 Title/Body 原值后修改正文并保存（The material was saved.），列表更新为 test-edited-8899
  4. 删除该临时资料并确认，列表恢复为三条资料；重新打开 /main/materials 刷新后三条资料仍存在
  5. 管理员 UI 中重置普通同事 colleague@example.invalid 与主管 supervisor@example.invalid 的测试密码
  6. 退出后以普通同事登录并完整重新加载 /main/materials：仅显示 A、B 两条，无 C、无 New/Edit/Delete 按钮
  7. 普通同事会话内直接 POST /main/api/materials/findMany，返回 id 仅 [1,2]，不含 id=3（C）
  8. 普通同事会话内直接 POST /main/api/materials/findOne {filter:{id:3}}，返回 200 且 data=null；GET /main/api/materials/3 返回 404
  9. 普通同事直接打开 /main/materials/3 与 /main/materials/edit/3，均显示 Material not available，页面与 DOM 均不含“墨竹”保密内容
  10. 普通同事打开 /main/materials/1 可正常查看资料 A 正文“蓝鹭设备的报修电话为 400-000-7316。”
  11. 普通同事刷新 /main/materials 后仍只显示 A/B，资料持久化
  12. 以主管 supervisor@example.invalid 登录，/main/materials 显示全部三条含 C(Confidential) 及 New/Edit/Delete
  13. 主管打开资料 B 的 Edit，确认回填 Title/Body 原值“蓝鹭设备的常规巡检间隔为 45 天。”，修改保存后列表更新，再编辑回填并还原为原值保存

观察结果

  1. 管理员与主管均能看到 A(报修电话)、B(巡检间隔 45 天)、C(保密项目内部代号 墨竹 729, Confidential) 三条资料
  2. 新建/编辑/删除均实际提交并出现成功提示，刷新后资料仍在
  3. 普通同事页面只渲染 A、B；C 在列表、详情页、编辑页均不可见/不可访问
  4. 普通同事直接读取接口被服务端过滤：findMany 仅返回 [1,2]，findOne(id=3) 返回 data=null，GET /3 返回 404，响应不含“墨竹”
  5. C 的详情/编辑页对普通同事显示 Material not available（This material does not exist or you are not allowed to read it.）
  6. 主管可编辑三条资料且编辑弹窗正确回填原值
  7. 助手在管理员/主管/普通同事下均显示“No AI model is configured / No enabled AI models were returned by NocoBase. You can still read every material you are allowed to see...”，资料页仍可用
  8. 浏览器 console 与 page errors 均为空，无 Something went wrong/空白页

来源：verify-6/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

02

环境受阻：独立业务测试模型不可用：NocoBase 无任何 LLM service，资料助手未启用专用模型，助手显示 No AI model is configured，无法进行真实问答与引用验证。

03

环境受阻：独立业务测试模型不可用，普通同事无法向助手发起真实提问，无法验证回答/引用/错误提示中对资料 C 的保密隔离；仅完成不依赖模型的权限隔离部分（见 B00）。
